# Complete run history

Snapshot exported on 2026-10-19: **388 run records**, **386 linked conservation figures**, and **4 supplementary figures**. Latest recorded run: 2026-10-19 22:16:57. 2 legacy rows have no saved figure (`NA`).

All table values and row order match the local snapshot; only Plot paths were converted. Images are lossless WebP with unchanged dimensions and verified identical RGBA pixels.

This data export includes experiments made with newer local program versions; it does not update the simulation code in this repository. The existing 2026-10-07 report describes its original subset, not a validation of every newer run.
